# Commands · M · part 8

1,417 entries, modularize to multi-backend. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **modularize**: Scans one or all ReactJS pages, extracts repeated layout blocks (header, footer, sidebar, navbar, etc.) into reusable components, and rewrites each page to import and use those components. Reuses existing components when they already exist. Never rewrites component logic — layout structure only. ([HLIS-Subhash-Shingadiya/codeninja](https://github.com/HLIS-Subhash-Shingadiya/codeninja/blob/HEAD/commands/modularize.workflow.md) · ★1)
- **modularize**: Analyze the app and recommend which parts could become workspace feature packages (Expo monorepo) — or whether to stay single-package. ([DentVega/expo-eas-aidlc](https://github.com/DentVega/expo-eas-aidlc/blob/HEAD/commands/modularize.md))
- **modularize**: Refactor a function into a well-structured, modular form (helpers, validation, error handling, docs). ([Lukee-bit/claude-config](https://github.com/Lukee-bit/claude-config/blob/HEAD/opencode/commands/modularize.md) · 2 copies)
- **modularize-areas**: Turn domain materials into a 5-question strategic 'show-me-the-money' report answered first, then candidate business areas grounded in those answers. Builds an evidence corpus, extracts ES images, scans sources for contradictory statements, and freezes structured artifacts behind manual gates. ([DomainDrivers/dd-ai](https://github.com/DomainDrivers/dd-ai/blob/HEAD/002/.claude/commands/modularize-areas.md) · ★9)
- **modularize-preflight**: Pre-flight gate — decide whether a domain is worth modularizing at all. Scans materials, asks three safeguard questions, produces a proceed/refuse verdict. ([DomainDrivers/dd-ai](https://github.com/DomainDrivers/dd-ai/blob/HEAD/002/.claude/commands/modularize-preflight.md) · ★9)

- **module-audit**: Pre-ship module audit pipeline. 7-stage matrix audit covering form components × 4 lenses + 10 cross-cutting axes + adversarial layer + SHA-anchored verification. Recon freezes one AUDIT_SHA; the Stage 4.5 verifier checks findings against that commit via git show (not HEAD) to catch snapshot incoherence. Use ONLY for module-completion sweeps with ≥3 form components. For single-page work use /page-design or /page-code; for changes meeting the project's Heavy-rigor trigger use /change-heavy; for every other change or FU cleanup use /cleanup-light. ([fire-farmer/red-line-toolkit](https://github.com/fire-farmer/red-line-toolkit/blob/HEAD/commands/module-audit.md))

- **mr-review-note**: 출력을 작성하기 전에 공통 지침 `core/rules/review-method-comparison.md`를 읽고 적용한다. 설치 환경에서는 `~/.agent-crew/rules/review-method-comparison.md`를 읽는다. 실제 방법명, 채택/권장/판단 보류 구분, 선택 이유, 얻는 점·감수할 점, 빈 줄과 `방법별 비교` 소제목, 후보별 설명·장단점, 확인 근거와 한계를 포함한다. 방법명이나 후보 개수를 고정하지 않는다. 방법 선택이 없는 항목에는 비교를 만들지 않는다. 이 형식은 보고서·분석·계획·인계·반영 노트의 서술에 적용하며, 원문 인용과 원시 증거, 프로토콜 필드, 상태·반영률·승인·실행 경계는 바꾸지 않는다. Use whenever the user says "check memory leak", "detect memory leaks", "memory too high", "OOM", "call Mr Yoo", "/mryoo-check-memory-leak", or asks why a Python app grows in memory. - **mtext.de**: So arbeitet AutoCADs MTEXT (Alias T): zwei Ecken oder Breite, Höhe, Ausrichten und Zeilenabstand, Absätze umbrochen in einem Objekt. Online gratis testen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/mtext.de.md))
